Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on navier-stokes structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 217. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 115 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the navier-stokes space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 217 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 115. QED.
